This constraint is the recipe's active ingredient. Without it, the same training answers RS questions correctly but in English, erases much of the base model's multilingual text ability, and diverges on one seed in three; sixteen alternatives, from training-free merging to prior orthogonality variants, fail the same way. MODL repairs every failure on every seed: answers are correct and in the target language 56-71% of the time, where the best alternative reaches 27% and most stay below 8%, text ability stays at the level of the untrained base, and on Spanish it surpasses Qwen2.5-VL-7B, with zero multilingual-multimodal data. A single five-language adapter retains English, Spanish, and Vietnamese at full strength across three seeds; non-Latin scripts remain an open boundary.

</details>


### [110] [TSS: Target-Side Sparsification for Speculative Decoding in Domain-Specific Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26100)
*Haibo Hu,Lianming Huang,Qiao Li,Nan Guan,Chun Jason Xue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TSS是一种用于推测性解码的目标侧稀疏化框架，在特定领域推理中，跳过部分目标层可以减少验证成本，同时提高草稿接受率并保持或改善下游任务性能。实验表明，TSS在不同领域、模型规模和推测性解码方法上均能取得一致的改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测性解码方法主要侧重于改进草稿模型，而目标模型通常保持密集不变。本文作者发现，在特定领域推理时，并不是整个目标模型需要进行全深验证，跳过某些目标层反而可以在减少验证成本的同时提高草稿接受率，甚至不影响（或改善）下游任务性能。

Method: TSS框架采用接受度和指标感知的广度搜索策略，探索多层跳过配置。这些配置存储在一个领域到配置映射表中，并由一个轻量级跳过控制器应用。这样，可以使用一个完整的密集目标模型支持多个稀疏验证路径，而无需重新训练或永久性参数剪枝。

Result: 实验结果显示，TSS在Spec-Bench上的多个领域、模型规模和推测性解码方法的设置下都取得了改进效果。特别是在翻译任务中，TSS将平均接受长度提高了67.8%，BLEU得分提升了80.9%，并增加了端到端的吞吐量，实现了1.68倍的速度提升。

Conclusion: TSS提供了一种有效的办法来优化推测性解码中的目标模型利用率，其方法能够显著提升模型的效率和性能，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference through collaboration between a lightweight draft model and a target verifier. Existing methods mainly improve the draft side, while the target model is typically kept dense and unchanged. We show that, under domain-specific inference, full-depth target verification is not always the optimal choice. Counter-intuitively, skipping selected target layers can reduce verification cost while simultaneously increasing draft acceptance and preserving, or even improving, downstream task performance. Based on this observation, we propose TSS, a target-side sparsification framework for speculative decoding. TSS employs an acceptance- and metric-aware breadth search to explore multi-layer skip configurations without imposing a fixed priority between the two objectives. The selected configurations are stored in a domain-to-configuration mapping and applied by a lightweight skip controller, allowing one complete target model to support multiple sparse verification paths without retraining or permanent parameter pruning. Experiments on Spec-Bench across multiple domains, model scales, and speculative decoding methods show consistent improvements in draft acceptance and downstream task performance. In Translation setting, TSS increases the average accept length from 2.70 to 4.53 (+67.8%), improves BLEU from 0.131 to 0.237 (+80.9%), and raises end-to-end throughput from 75.6 to 127.3 tokens/s, corresponding to a 1.68X speedup.

</details>


### [111] [Differentiable Fuzzy Inference Layer: A Monotone, Compositional Ordinal Reasoning Head for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26113)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，标准分类头在处理按顺序分类时表现不佳，引入了Differentiable Fuzzy Inference Layer (DFIL) 来解决此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在处理如 'most of most students passed' 这样需要复杂推理的顺序类别时表现不佳，研究动机在于克服这一局限并提升准确性。

Method: 为解决标准分类模型的不足，研究引入了一个新的模块DFIL，该模块通过标准分类头和标量瓶颈分支相结合的方式，支持单调性和组成性推理。

Result: DFIL 模型在多项顺序自然语言任务中表现优异，特别是在不同语言模型家族中均体现出良好的性能。

Conclusion: 此研究通过引入DFIL模块，有效改善了在处理顺序类别方面的性能，验证了DFIL在复杂推理任务中的潜力。

Abstract: A state-of-the-art language model asked to interpret "most of most students passed" typically answers "most," though composing two instances of "most" yields a proportion closer to "some." We trace this failure to an architectural choice rather than a data deficit: standard classifier heads treat ordinal categories as independent labels, with no mechanism to respect their natural ordering or compose them algebraically. We introduce the Differentiable Fuzzy Inference Layer (DFIL), a dual-path prediction head pairing a standard classifier with a scalar-bottlenecked branch grounded in a bank of ordered membership functions. DFIL supplies two structural primitives that a label-only head cannot inherit: monotonicity in the underlying quantity, and compositional reasoning via t-norm operations without any compositional training data. The scalar branch additionally provides an interpretable interface for analyzing residual errors. We instantiate DFIL on ordinal natural-language tasks across diverse LLM families.

</details>


### [112] [Magnitude Profile Pruning: Calibration-Free Structured Attention Head Removal for Transformer Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26177)
*Kasun Dewage,Marianna Pensky,Heranga K. Rathnasekara,Suranadi De Silva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Magnitude Profile (MP) 是一种无需训练即可评估头部重要性的统计方法，通过统计异常检测对权重行范数进行分析，识别并删减冗余的注意力头部。MP-G 变体适用于 Grouped Query Attention (GQA)，保持了高效的压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的注意力头部重要性评估方法依赖于校准数据、梯度计算或海森矩阵估计，这增加了额外的开销，使方法依赖于特定的数据集。本研究旨在提供一种基于重量直接统计的方法，以简化注意力头部裁剪，提高压缩效率。

Method: MP 方法通过计算注意力头部的权重行范数并应用统计异常检测来确定需裁剪的头部；而 MP-G 方法针对共享关键字值组的 Attention 头部设计，将组得分按关联查询头部进行分布，从而适应 Grouped Query Attention (GQA) 模型的特殊需求。

Result: 在 WikiText-2 数据集的 12.5%-50% 头部稀疏度下，MP-G 在大模型如 OPT-6.7B 和 RoBERTa-large 上的表现尤为出色，12.5% 稀疏度下分别为 18.46 和 7.27 的困惑度，50% 稀疏度下分别为 27.87 和 152.0 的困惑度，优于依赖校准的数据方法。此外，在 50% 头部裁剪的情况下，头部裁剪可带来高达 16% 参数减少和 50% 注意力 FLOP 节约。

Conclusion: 基于权重的统计方法（MP 和 MP-G）能够匹配甚至超越依赖于特定数据集的方法，提供了灵活且高效的 Transformer 压缩方案。这一发现为注意力头部修剪提供了一种无需额外成本的实用标准。

Abstract: Structured pruning of attention heads provides a hardware-friendly way to compress Transformer language models. However, existing methods for measuring head-level importance require calibration data, gradient computation, or Hessian estimation. These requirements add extra overhead and make the methods depend on the data. Our work presents Magnitude Profile (MP) scoring, a training-free criterion for head importance that identifies dispensable heads through statistical outlier detection on weight row norms. Heads whose projection weights fall within the population bulk are pruned, while heads exhibiting outlier norms, which carry disproportionate representational capacity, are preserved. Our work further gives MP-G, a variant that handles Grouped Query Attention (GQA) by distributing shared key-value group scores across associated query heads. Across five models evaluated on WikiText-2 perplexity at 12.5%-50% head sparsity, MP-G achieves the best perplexity on OPT-6.7B at all sparsity levels (18.46 at 12.5%, 27.87 at 25%, 152.0 at 50%). MP-G also gives the best results on RoBERTa-large at 12.5% and 25% sparsity, with perplexity values of 7.27 and 10.28, outperforming calibration-dependent baselines including Wanda-Head, SparseGPT-Head, and Gradient-Head. It requires zero forward passes, calibration samples, or gradient computation. At 50% sparsity, head pruning yields up to 16% parameter reduction with 50% attention FLOP savings. Our results show that weight-only statistical scoring can match or outperform data-dependent methods for structured head pruning, providing a practical, zero-cost criterion for Transformer compression.

</details>


### [113] [Modality-Gated Deep Adapters: Adding a Modality to a Frozen Embedding Model with Exact Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26182)
*Abdul Basit Tonmoy,Kazi Fardinul Hoque,Md. Shahrier Islam Arham,Arman Luthra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的方法，即模态门控深度适配器，用于扩展多模态嵌入模型到新模态而不影响现有输出。这种方法通过将模态门控的深适配器连接到冻结的多模态嵌入LLM的每一解码器层，并仅在相应的模态被编码时执行，从而实现这一点。研究展示了这种方法在音频和热成像场景中的性能提升，并在预训练的模型上验证了其不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效扩展方法会改变已有模型的输出，这会破坏已存储的嵌入。为了解决这个问题，提出了模态门控深度适配器，该方法旨在保持现有模型输出不变，同时能够处理新的模态。

Method: 方法涉及将模态门控的深适配器连接到冻结的多模态嵌入LLM的每一解码器层。这些适配器以模态为组组织，在相应的模态被编码时仅执行。这种设计保证了输入的计算路径不会改变，并且适配器之间有精确的隔离。研究中还展示了如何通过控制投入模型的模态来实现这一点，从而保持原有模型输出的不变性。

Result: 研究展示了在音频到文本和热成像到文本任务中，模态门控深度适配器的有效性。实验表明，相比于对照组，在相同的训练条件下，研究中提出的音频适配器能够在不同种子下显著提升音频到文本检索的准确性，增幅在3.4%到5.4%之间。热成像适配器则能够大幅度提高热成像到文本检索的准确性，从0.224提升到0.785。这些结果通过对比实验和直接比较外接的音频编码器来进一步证明。值得注意的是，编码器的替换实验表明，内部增加的适配器成功提供了所需的容量，而无需额外资源。

Conclusion: 研究提出并验证了模态门控深度适配器，这是一种有效的方法，确保在扩展多模态嵌入模型到新模态时，现有的嵌入和模型输出保持不变。该方法验证了在不同任务和数据集中的普遍适用性，并展示了其在实际应用中的优势。

Abstract: Multimodal embedding models are deployed at scale: retrieval indices, benchmark results, and behavioral audits all depend on the base model's exact outputs. Extending such a model to a new modality with existing parameter-efficient methods silently changes those outputs; LoRA-style adaptation rewrites the text path whether or not the weights are merged, invalidating every stored embedding. We propose modality-gated deep adapters: bottleneck adapters attached to every decoder layer of a frozen multimodal embedding LLM, grouped into per-modality packs that execute only while their own modality is being encoded. The result is a modality added with zero change to existing outputs: inputs no pack claims traverse the base model's own computation graph, bit-for-bit unchanged, and co-loaded packs compose with an exact-zero isolation matrix. Both properties are stated as propositions, hold after arbitrary training rather than only at initialization, require no task labels or routing metadata at inference, and are verified by exact-equality tests on the released checkpoints. On one frozen 2B base, the audio pack (injected as connector tokens) improves audio-to-text R@10 by +3.4 to +5.4 points over an identically trained control, positive at every seed and reproduced at eleven times the data; the thermal pack, reusing the base's own frozen vision path, clears its pre-registered acceptance gate roughly sevenfold at every seed and lifts thermal-to-text R@10 from 0.224 to 0.785. An encoder swap locates the missing capacity: an external audio encoder that outranks Whisper-family encoders in CLAP-style comparisons loses by 16 R@10 points inside the frozen LLM, so the capacity belongs in the layers, exactly where the gated adapters place it. We release the audio model, the thermal pack, and the training, evaluation and invariance suites: models at huggingface.co/EximiusLabs, code on GitHub.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond Static Charts: Can Language and Vision Language Models Generate Interactive Data Visualization Interfaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26208)
*Mizanur Rahman,Aaryaman Kartha,Enamul Hoque Prince*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了VIS-GEN基准测试，评估LLMs/VLMs生成自然语言查询驱动的交互式数据可视化的性能，并提出了一种结构化的多阶段界面生成框架以提高模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现实时数据分析，传统静态图表已无法满足需求，研究旨在衡量和改进LLMs/VLMs生成交互式数据可视化界面的能力。

Method: 研究设计了VIS-GEN基准测试，包含3,042个样本，涵盖从数据过滤到复杂编辑操作的各种分析意图，还提出了拆分任务为可视化设计表示、界面候选生成、约束意识批评和自我修正的多阶段框架。

Result: 在14个最新基准模型上进行测试，发现性能差异显著，特别是在处理隐含意图、多种交互选择和复杂编辑操作的查询时表现不佳。提出的多阶段框架将模型通过率提高了15.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究指出，LLMs/VLMs生成交互式数据可视化界面仍存在挑战，但通过改进可以实现更可靠的语言驱动型交互式数据可视化系统。

Abstract: Data visualization is central to analytical reasoning, but real-world analysis increasingly requires language-driven interactive interfaces rather than static charts. Although recent large language and vision language models (LLMs/VLMs) have shown promise in generating static charts from natural language, their ability to generate interactive data visualization interfaces remains largely unexplored due to the lack of benchmarks. We introduce VIS-GEN, a benchmark for evaluating how well LLMs/VLMs can generate interactive visualization interfaces from natural language queries. VIS-GEN comprises 3,042 samples covering diverse analytical intents, including data filtering, temporal analysis, and visualization editing, each paired with dataset metadata and natural language queries that are designed to reflect realistic, goal driven data exploration scenarios. We benchmark 14 state-of-the-art open-source and closed-source LLMs/VLMs, revealing large performance gaps and frequent failures on queries involving implicit intent, multiple interaction alternatives, and complex editing operations, highlighting interactive interface generation as a key open challenge beyond static chart synthesis. To address this, we propose a structured multi stage interface generation framework that decomposes the task into visualization design representation, generation of multiple interface candidates, constraint-aware critique, and self-refinement. This approach improves the best models pass rate by 15.9 percentage points, demonstrating a practical path toward more reliable language-driven interactive visualization systems. We release VIS-GEN at https://github.com/vis-nlp/VIS-GEN.

</details>


### [115] [Same Chart, Different Story: Bias in Vision-Language Chart Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26210)
*Mizanur Rahman,Huan Wu,Arash Asgari,Enamul Hoque Prince,Laleh Seyyed-Kalantari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究介绍了ChartBias，这是一个用于评估视觉-语言模型在解释图表时跨社会维度偏见的首个基准。研究揭示了三种广泛的失败模式，并提出了一种多智能体缓解框架来减少这些偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于视觉-语言模型在生成图表解释时可能会因为参考的社会群体不同而产生不同的叙述，从而可能固化刻板印象并误导决策，因此需要一个评估这种偏见的基准。ChartBias提供了一种系统的方法来审计视觉-语言模型在图表解释中的偏见。

Method: 研究通过创建一个包含820个手工筛选的真实图表的基准，每个图表有六个属性（种族、收入、年龄、宗教、移民状态和性别），共计4,319个图表-属性实例和8,638对生成，来评估视觉-语言模型在图表解释中的表现。研究使用了12个专有和开源的模型，总计155,484个模型响应，分析了三种广泛存在的失败模式，并提出了一个多智能体缓解框架。

Result: 研究发现了三种广泛的模型失败模式：叙述转换（相同图表不同的叙述）、群体幻觉（没有证据将图表与一个群体关联）和偏好极性（有利的趋势通常与一个特定群体相关）。所提出的多智能体缓解框架在减少叙述转换的同时保持了基于图表的推理。

Conclusion: 研究强调，评估图表理解不仅需要衡量准确性，还需要衡量公平性和一致性跨社会群体。此外，研究还提出了一个可以公开使用的多智能体缓解框架，并将其发布在了GitHub上。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to interpret charts and generate natural-language explanations for socially consequential data. However, they may produce different narratives for the same chart when only the referenced social group changes, reinforcing stereotypes and misleading decisions. Despite these risks, no benchmark exists for systematically evaluating bias in chart interpretation across social dimensions. We introduce ChartBias, the first benchmark for auditing bias in VLM-based chart interpretation. ChartBias contains 820 manually curated real-world charts spanning six attributes: race, income, age, religion, immigration status, and gender, yielding 4,319 valid chart, attribute instances and 8,638 paired generations where the chart is fixed and only the group term is swapped. Across 12 proprietary and open-source VLMs, totaling 155,484 model responses, we find three widespread failure modes: narrative shift (same chart, different narratives), group hallucination (assigning a chart to a group without evidence), and preference polarity (favourable trends often linked to one group). We further propose a multi-agent mitigation framework that serves as a strong baseline by separating chart-grounded evidence extraction from group-conditioned generation and using a counterfactual judge to verify that group-driven differences are supported by the chart. The framework substantially reduces narrative shift while preserving chart-grounded reasoning. Our findings show that evaluating chart understanding requires measuring not only accuracy, but also fairness and consistency across social groups. We release ChartBias at https://github.com/vis-nlp/ChartBiasBench.

</details>


### [116] [Damage Predicts Recovery: When Calibration Data Matters in Compressing Financial LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26241)
*Junyi Ye,Mengjia Yu,Debapriya Hazra,Guiling Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究表明，在某些情况下，特定领域的校准数据可以改进剪枝导致的性能损失，而量化通常不会显著影响任务性能。应首先测量任务特定的压缩损害，并仅在损伤较大时才构建专门的校准数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨后训练量化和剪枝策略在不同压缩水平下的影响，特别是在专门领域（如金融）下的表现

Method: 跨两种模型家族、六种压缩配置、三种标记匹配的校准数据集和十种金融分类和数字问答任务进行测试

Result: 量化在大多数情况下能够保持任务性能，而剪枝则显著降低数字问答准确性。FinMix（金融任务示例的混合）在损坏情况下能够显著恢复性能。

Conclusion: 结论是，在任务特定压缩损害较小的情况下，采用一般的校准数据足以，而在较大损害下应使用专门的金融领域校准数据。

Abstract: Post-training quantization and pruning rely on a small calibration corpus. Whether specialized domains such as finance require domain-matched calibration data remains unsettled. We argue that the answer depends on the task-level damage caused by compression rather than on domain mismatch. If compression preserves the target capability, changing the calibration corpus has little effect. If compression causes large losses, task-formatted calibration can recover part of the loss. We test this hypothesis across two model families, six compression configurations, three token-matched calibration corpora, and ten financial classification and numerical question-answering tasks. The results support this hypothesis. Quantization largely preserves task performance, and calibration choice has little effect in this case. Pruning reduces numerical QA accuracy by over 40 points. In these damaged settings, another generic corpus does not help, while FinMix, a mixture of financial task examples, recovers a large part of the loss. The link between damage and recovery holds across model families and scales. These findings support a practical rule. Measure task-specific compression damage first, and construct specialized calibration data only when the damage is large.

</details>


### [117] [PACE-dLLM: Elastic Block Decoding via Confidence Cliff Estimation for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26249)
*Xiaocheng Lu,Shuhan Guo,Ziyue Ma,Jie Zhang,Jian Liu,Jingcai Guo,Haoxuan Che,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PACE-dLLM 提出了一个精确加速策略，通过适应性设置前瞻窗口，实现了在多个基准测试中生成质量和加速比的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的加速策略如阻尼检测和预测长度策略难以精确地同时确定前瞻窗口和承诺令牌的数量。作者发现模型自身的每步置信度信息已编码了所需信息，提出了PACE-dLLM，该方法在每步直接适应性确定前瞻窗口，提高了效率。

Method: PACE-dLLM 通过参数化的悬崖模型实现前瞻窗口的动态调整，每个步骤使用饱和点设置前瞻窗口，并用独立的阈值确定令牌承诺。

Result: 在四个推理和代码基准测试中，PACE-dLLM 在 Open-LLM 语言模型上都达到了最高的平均准确率，分别在 LLaDA 和 Dream 模型上实现了最多 5.23 倍和 3.06 倍的平均实际加速比，甚至在数学任务中高达 8.52 倍。

Conclusion: PACE-dLLM 提供了一种在保持语言建模质量的同时大幅提升生成效率的方法，拓展了质量-吞吐量的帕累托前沿。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs), such as LLaDA and Dream, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) LLMs in generation quality while supporting native parallel decoding. A standard acceleration strategy is block-wise decoding, where each forward pass predicts a block of length B and commits high-confidence tokens. However, B couples two distinct decisions: the look-ahead horizon and the number of tokens to commit. Existing accelerators address this limitation through indirect heuristics, such as volatility tracking, delimiter detection, and learned scoring. In contrast, we show that the required information is already encoded in the model's own per-step confidence: in-window confidence typically follows a context-dependent cliff, whose saturation point directly identifies the appropriate look-ahead horizon. We propose PACE-dLLM, which fits this parametric cliff in closed form at each step, sets the next horizon by its saturation point, and uses an independent confidence threshold for token commitment. Under a saturated-yield abstraction, we show that the cliff-anchored horizon is the smallest horizon attaining maximal useful per-pass yield: fixed horizons that undershoot it incur a worse asymptotic NFE rate, while overshooting adds no useful yield. On four reasoning and code benchmarks, PACE-dLLM achieves the best average accuracy on both open-source dLLM backbones, with average wall-clock speedups of 5.23x on LLaDA and 3.06x on Dream (up to 8.52x on math) over the unaccelerated semi-AR baseline, advancing the quality-throughput Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [118] [CHiME-9 ECHI: A Machine Learning Challenge for Enhancing Conversations to Address Hearing Impairment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26306)
*Robert Sutherland,Thomas Kuebert,Marko Lugger,Stefan Petrausch,Eline Borch Petersen,Juan Azcarreta Ortiz,Buye Xu,Stefan Goetze,Jon Barker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CHiME-9挑战赛的任务和结果，旨在通过增强对话来解决听力障碍问题。挑战涉及嘈杂的咖啡厅环境中四人对话，参与者提供带有Meta Aria眼镜和助听器麦克风的音频记录，以及对话参与者的干净语音样本。目标是从嘈杂的多通道录音中提取对话伙伴的语音，以提高语音的可理解性和质量，使用客观指标和主观听力测试进行评估。结果表明，虽然客观指标未反映听众性能，但顶尖系统在可理解性与质量评分方面相较于基线有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在解决听力障碍环境下对话增强问题，改善在噪音和干扰中的对话质量，通过多队参与的比赛提升技术解决方案。

Method: 采用多队提交的方法，提供了Meta Aria眼镜和助听器麦克风录制的音频记录，以及干净的语音样本。参与者需要开发系统从嘈杂的多通道录音中提取对话伙伴的语音，通过客观和主观测试评估。

Result: 结果显示，尽管客观指标没有反映出听众的真实表现，但领先的系统在智能性与质量评分上相比挑战基线有显著的改进。

Conclusion: 本文总结了CHiME-9挑战赛的提交结果，展示了尖端技术在对话增强方面取得的进展，尤其是在改善对话质量和可理解性方面的成就。

Abstract: This work presents the task and results of the CHiME-9 challenge for Enhancing Conversations to address Hearing Impairment. The challenge considers the scenario of four-party conversations in a noisy, cafeteria-style environment with interfering speech sources and sound effects. Participants are provided with audio recordings made with Meta Aria glasses and hearing aid microphones, and clean speech samples of the conversation participants. The task is to extract the speech of the conversation partners from the noisy multi-channel recordings with the goal of improving the intelligibility and quality of the speech, evaluated using objective metrics and subjective listening tests. This paper reviews submissions from seven teams and ranks them on a combination of subjective intelligibility and quality. Results show that while the objective metrics do not reflect listener performance, the top systems were able to make substantial improvements over the challenge baseline in both intelligibility and quality ratings.

</details>


### [119] [Designing and Analysing Argument Mining Pipelines: Towards a Comprehensive Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26338)
*Siddharth Bhargava,Sara Tonelli,Patricia Martín-Rodilla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种初步元研究，系统地回顾了多项最新的端到端论证挖掘工作，并通过三重视角框架对其进行分析，从而更好地理解这些架构如何建模、计算以及整合领域知识。该框架为未来研究提供了方法论中心的描述，有助于深入理解并系统比较不同方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的论证挖掘方法在概念化任务方面存在差异，使得直接比较这些方法难以进行。因此，研究者提出了一种三重视角的分析框架，旨在提供更细致、任务级别的分析，以便进行清晰比较和评估。

Method: 通过系统回顾最新的端到端论证挖掘工作，并采用三重视角框架（语言学、计算和领域视角）进行分析来理解各种架构如何建模和计算论证结构。进一步提出了一种在语言学和计算视角上的通用设计，以展示关键任务如何进行论证结构的建模和计算。

Result: 提出的三重视角框架为未来研究提供了方法论中心的描述，有助于更深入地理解不同论证挖掘方法，并且有助于进行更系统的比较。

Conclusion: 该研究为论证挖掘领域提供了新的分析方法，使得不同方法之间的比较更加清晰和系统化。

Abstract: Argument Mining (AM) transforms natural language into its underlying argument structures. This transformation is typically realized through a sequence of AM tasks that form an end-to-end AM pipeline. However, AM approaches often differ in how they conceptualize these tasks, making direct comparisons between them difficult and opaque. This calls for a more nuanced, task-level analysis of AM approaches to enable clearer comparison and assessment.
  This work presents a preliminary meta-study that systematically reviews several state-of-the-art end-to-end AM works and analyzes their pipelines through a triple-perspective framework---a linguistic, computational and domain perspective---to understand how the pipelines model arguments as structures, computes them, and integrates domain knowledge. We further propose a general design to the linguistic and computational perspectives, illustrating how key AM tasks are designed for modeling and computation of argument structures. Our proposed framework lays the groundwork for methodology-centered descriptions across AM approaches, facilitating deeper understanding and more systematic comparisons in future research.

</details>


### [120] [Blaming Across the Aisle: Political Contrasting and Blame Attribution in the Danish Parliament](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26346)
*Markus Lundsfryd Jensen,Rune Egeskov Trust,Kenneth Christian Enevoldsen,Sara Kolding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析1997年至2026年间丹麦议会的责备归属情况，发现责备呈现出最初下降后在2019年至2026年间显著增加的趋势。政府地位对责备归属有显著影响，反对党比执政党更常责备他人，且这种影响程度受政治极化程度的影响，尤其是在右翼政党中更为显著。研究表明，政治语言的加剧反映了意识形态不对称的强化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在填补关于政治话语愈发敌对的证据不足的问题，通过分析丹麦议会中责备归属的变化趋势，探讨政治话语中责备现象的演变及其背后的意识形态因素。

Method: 研究采用了自建的BlameBERT分类器(F1分数为0.80)，并结合多级统计建模方法，对1997年至2026年间丹麦议会的议题进行分析。分类器通过一个标注高效且适用于低至中资源语言的管道构建。

Result: 研究结果显示，责备现象经历了初始下降随后在2019年至2026年间显著增加。政府地位对责备倾向有显著影响，反对党比执政党更倾向于责备对手。这一影响的程度受到政党政治极化程度的影响，在右翼政党中尤为显著。此外，近年来政治派别和意识形态极化之间的互动关系增强，表明政治责备氛围在右翼政治光谱中更加尖锐。

Conclusion: 研究表明，政治语言的加剧并非普遍性的语言演化，而是具有意识形态不对称性的强化。

Abstract: Political discourse is widely perceived to be growing more hostile, yet robust evidence remains scarce. This study examines blame attribution in the Danish Parliament from 1997 to 2026, combining a purpose-built classifier, BlameBERT (F1: 0.80), with multilevel statistical modeling. The classifier is constructed using an annotation-efficient pipeline for blame attribution in low-to-mid resource languages. The results reveal a banana-shaped trajectory, with blame declining until around 2016 before entering a significant and sustained increase in recent years (2019-2026). Government status consistently influenced blame attribution - an effect we term political contrasting - with opposition parties blaming substantially more than governing parties. This effect was moderated by ideology: The blame-dampening effect of governing was less pronounced among right-wing parties, and ideological extremity amplified blame more strongly on the right. In recent years, the interaction between political wing and ideological extremity intensified, suggesting an ideological hardening of the blame rhetoric concentrated on the right of the political spectrum. Taken together, these patterns suggest that the perceived rise in harsh political language reflects not merely a general rhetorical drift, but an ideologically asymmetric hardening of political discourse. A sensitivity analysis showed that the conclusions were robust to varying classification thresholds.

</details>


### [121] [TransBERT: A Framework for Synthetic Translation in Domain-Specific Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26347)
*Julien Knafou,Luc Mottin,Anaïs Mottaz,Alexandre Flament,Patrick Ruch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为TransBERT的新框架，通过合成翻译文本进行预训练，并提供了TransCorpus翻译工具包和相关数据集，从而展示在低资源语言/领域对中构建高质量NLP资源的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自然语言处理工具主要基于英语数据，这限制了其在非英语语言领域的应用。而针对特定领域（如医学）的非英语数据稀缺，因此需要开发新的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 研究团队开发了TransBERT框架，使用合成翻译的数据进行预训练。此外还开发了TransCorpus工具包，可以大量产生合成翻译文本。

Result: 在医学领域（法语）的下游任务中，使用合成翻译数据进行预训练可以获得与使用真实翻译数据相当的效果。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在高资源语言向低资源语言的翻译方向中，合成翻译是一种可行的方法，有助于构建高质量的NLP资源。

Abstract: The scarcity of non-English language data in specialized domains significantly limits the development of effective Natural Language Processing (NLP) tools. We present TransBERT, a novel framework for pre-training language models using exclusively synthetically translated text, and introduce TransCorpus, a scalable translation toolkit. Focusing on the life sciences domain in French, our approach demonstrates that state-of-the-art performance on various downstream tasks can be achieved solely by leveraging synthetically translated data. We release the TransCorpus toolkit, the TransCorpus-bio-fr corpus (36.4GB of French life sciences text), TransBERT-bio-fr, its associated pre-trained language model and reproducible code for both pre-training and fine-tuning. Our results highlight the viability of synthetic translation in a high-resource translation direction for building high-quality NLP resources in low-resource language/domain pairs.

</details>


### [122] [HySparse2: Hybrid Sparse Attention with Two-Level KV Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26368)
*Jianyu Wei,Yizhao Gao,Qihao Zhang,Shimao Chen,Zhengju Tang,Yu Cheng,Shengjie Zhou,Zihan Jiang,Yifan Song,Hailin Zhang,Liang Zhao,Bo Yang,Gang Wang,Shijie Cao,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HySparse2 是一种双层次稀疏注意力架构，结合了K/V桥接和K/V重用设计，提高长期上下文检索和多轮代理任务性能，同时减少预填充计算和K/V缓存存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对长上下文和多轮代理的需求，传统的稀疏注意力机制需要改进，以更高效地处理长期的上下文信息。

Method: HySparse2 使用双层次KV共享机制，外层通过YOCO风格的自解码器和交叉解码器结构，内层通过K/V重用设计改进，具体包括基于token的稀疏性和滑动窗口的选择。

Result: HySparse2 在80B-A3B MoE模型上，在长期上下文检索和多轮代理任务中优于HySparse和Hybrid SWA，同时减少了预填充计算和K/V缓存存储。

Conclusion: HySparse2 通过改进稀疏注意力机制，提高效率并适应长上下文和多轮代理任务，并且在实际应用中展示了其性能优势。

Abstract: Long-horizon and multi-turn agents typically generate short actions and process long observations from tools and environments. This growing context demands efficient prefill, compact KV-cache storage, and accurate long-context retrieval. To meet these demands, we introduce HySparse2, a hybrid sparse attention architecture with two-level KV sharing. At the outer level, KV Bridging adopts a YOCO-style self-decoder and cross-decoder structure, but bridges only full-attention layers. The self-decoder uses hybrid sliding-window attention (SWA), while the cross-decoder uses hybrid sparse attention. The KV caches for full-attention layers in the cross-decoder are generated from the hidden states of full-attention layers in the self-decoder. At the inner level, HySparse2 retains HySparse's core KV Reuse design with two refinements. First, it replaces block-level sparsity with token-level sparsity for finer long-context retrieval. Second, it removes the separate SWA branch from sparse layers and instead forces a sliding window of recent tokens into the sparse selection. This two-level KV sharing allows all cross-decoder KV caches to be constructed from self-decoder hidden states. Prefill can therefore exit after the self-decoder, skipping all cross-decoder layers. On an 80B-A3B MoE model, HySparse2 outperforms HySparse and Hybrid SWA on long-context retrieval and multi-turn agentic tasks, while substantially reducing prefill computation and KV-cache storage.

</details>


### [123] [Layout-Guided Masking for GROBID: Lightweight Structural Gains in Large-Scale Scientific PDF Ingestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26381)
*Luca Foppiano,Sana Khamassi,Vipul Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于CPU的轻量级方案，结合GROBID和文本区域检测，提升了学术文章结构化处理的性能，特别是在表格检测和段落识别方面表现优异，同时成本明显低于使用GPU的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有基于视觉的解析器需要GPU且可能引入噪声，因此开发了一种基于CPU的轻量级文本区域检测方案，以提高学术文章的结构化处理效率并降低成本。

Method: 方案结合了基于CPU的GROBID进行结构化处理和一个轻量级的文本区域检测器定位图像、表格和副文本区域（如标题、页脚和页码），并将其标记为类型化的区域掩码。检测到的区域信息被传送给GROBID中针对特定区域优化的模型，或者丢弃。

Result: 在两个PMC语料库（生物信息学和材料科学）上，与GROBID相比，该方法大多数指标有所改善，尤其是在材料科学语料库中段落召回率提高了8.6%，并且在外部Table-BRGM基准测试中，表格检测的F1分数从0.16提高至0.94，结构也得到了良好恢复（GriTS-Top为0.78）。此外，该方法在段落精度和节检测方面优于其他基于视觉的系统，并且在字符错误率上与最佳GPU解析器相近。此外，该系统比最便宜的基于GPU的系统（Docling）成本低2.7至3.2倍，并且比生成式解析器低10至14倍。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法能够有效地提高学术文章结构化处理的精度和效率，并且成本更低，能够在不使用GPU的情况下完成高质量的文本和表格数据提取任务。

Abstract: Transforming scholarly PDFs into machine-readable fulltext remains a bottleneck for large-scale information systems. Recent vision-based parsers improve accuracy, but need GPUs and may introduce noise into the extracted text. GROBID, a modular font-stream parser running on CPU, is the de-facto standard for structuring scientific articles and underpins several of the largest open scholarly corpora. We pair it with a lightweight CPU detector localising figure, table, and paratext (header, footer, page number) regions, encoded as typed-area masks whose tokens are routed to GROBID's specialised models or discarded. On two PMC corpora, Bioinformatics (1,926 articles) and Materials Science (2,595), scored against JATS with a section-aware structural protocol, our extension improves over plain GROBID on most metrics (NS $+0.025$/$+0.013$; $+0.086$ paragraph recall on Materials Science, $d_z{=}1.08$), and caption-linked figure recovery improves on both corpora. On the external Table-BRGM benchmark, table detection recovers F1 $0.16 \to 0.94$ and table structure follows (GriTS-Top $0.27 \to 0.78$, below the strongest GPU system). On body text, against four vision-based systems (Docling, MinerU, olmOCR, dots.ocr), it has the best paragraph precision on both corpora, the best section detection on Materials Science, and a character error rate within 0.004 of the best GPU parser. End-to-end on CPU, it costs $2.7$--$3.2\times$ less than the cheapest GPU system (Docling) and $10$--$14\times$ less than generative parsers.

</details>


### [124] [Combining Hierarchical Cognitive Process with Process Supervision for Interpretable Scene Safety Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26399)
*Zhiyun Jiang,Hanyong Wang,Binbin Liang,Yu Xie,Zhengjie Wang,Menglong Yang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的场景安全理解方法，通过构建层次认知安全结构和过程监督框架，基于多步推理构建基准数据集，并利用低秩适应（LoRA）和专家混合（MoE）策略提升模型的解释性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以提供可解释性，限制了在关键应用中的可靠性。本文旨在通过引入认知模型和过程监督，使其与人类认知过程更相似，以提高场景安全理解的解释性和可靠性。

Method: 首先构建层次认知安全结构，基于多步推理构建数据集；设计模块化和灵活的过程监督框架，使用低秩适应（LoRA）和混合专家（MoE）策略针对多个子推理过程进行建模。并利用大型语言模型（LLMs）作为核心架构。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在解释性和性能方面明显优于传统方法。数据集和过程监督框架为大型语言模型增强了安全推理能力和允许细化地分析中间推理步骤。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过认知建模和过程监督能够显著提高场景安全理解的解释性和性能，为关键领域的应用提供了新方法。

Abstract: Scene safety understanding plays a life-or-death role in situational awareness in various critical domains. Traditional methods that rely on learning direct mappings between scenes and safety levels often lack interpretability, limiting their reliability in critical applications. An effective approach to overcoming this challenge lies in interpreting human cognitive processes and equipping machine models with analogous cognitive capabilities. This work explores an effective way of integrating scene safety cognitive process modeling and process supervision. Specifically, we first construct a hierarchical cognitive safety structure, which motivates the development of a novel, high-quality scene safety understanding dataset based on multi-step reasoning with process labels. This dataset serves both as a benchmark and a resource to improve the safety reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), while also enabling a granular analysis of intermediate reasoning steps through information flow and saliency-based techniques. Building upon this foundation, we introduce a modular and flexible process supervision framework that reflects the hierarchical nature of human cognition. This framework leverages LLMs as the core architecture and incorporates Low-Rank Adaptation(LoRA) and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) strategies to enable specialization and collaboration among expert modules, each tasked with specific sub-processes of the overall reasoning chain. Systematic experimental evaluations and analyses confirm that our framework exhibits superior interpretability and performance characteristics compared to traditional approaches.

</details>


### [125] [Enriching Speech Emotion Representations with Conversational Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26422)
*Arthur Peuvot,Romaric Besançon,Gaël de Chalendar,Bianca Vieru,Ioana Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 介绍了ACERT模块，用于捕捉口语交互中情绪演变，并在多个数据集上优于当前最先进的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多在单元水平上预测情绪，但忽视了对话背景下的情绪流动和参与者互动。

Method: ACERT模块通过整合灵活长度的对话背景窗口来更好地捕捉情感演变。

Result: 在IEMOCAP数据集上优于SOTA，首次在SAFE数据集上建立上下文感知基准，并在MELD数据集上取得了强劲结果。

Conclusion: ACERT的增益来自于情感和对话一致性，而不是说话者身份或声学条件。

Abstract: Detecting emotions is necessary for building systems that can accurately and adaptively interact with humans. Speech Emotion Recognition (SER) has become an important research focus to develop intelligent spoken interfaces. However, most studies predict emotions at the utterance level, ignoring the conversational context, along with the emotional flow and speaker interactions it carries. In this paper, we introduce ACERT (Averaged Contextual Emotion Representation through Time), a module that integrates a flexible-length window of conversational context to better capture emotional evolution in spoken interactions. To evaluate the robustness of this method, we conducted experiments on datasets spanning diverse emotionally expressive styles and contexts. ACERT outperforms current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches on IEMOCAP, establishes the first context-aware benchmark on SAFE, and obtains strong results on MELD for unweighted, class-balanced metrics. Ablation studies show that ACERT's gains come from emotional and conversational continuity, rather than from speaker identity or acoustic conditions.

</details>


### [126] [How to Estimate Whether You Have Found Several Needles in a Haystack: Measuring Calibration in Multi-Label Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26468)
*Sophie Henning,Georg Hofmann,Alexander Schulte,Alexander Fraser,Annemarie Friedrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个新的分箱方案用于多标签分类任务中的置信度校准误差估计，该方案能够更准确地评估置信度评分的校准情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的置信度校准方法主要针对二分类或多分类任务，但在处理多标签分类任务时表现不足，特别是对于大量未标注标签的情况。

Method: 研究提出了一种新的分箱策略，旨在为每一类权重相等地分配正标签和负标签，以更准确地估计每个类别的校准误差。

Result: 实验表明，该新分箱方法在层次化和极端多标签分类任务中能够提供更加有意义的校准误差估计，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为多标签分类任务提供了一种新的评价度量标准，促进了进一步的研究，特别是针对大型语言模型的多标签预测置信度校准难题。

Abstract: A key factor in deciding whether to trust an automatic prediction is its confidence score, which should be calibrated to match the actual probability of the prediction being correct. Most confidence calibration metrics target binary or multi-class tasks, while multi-label calibration remains largely underexplored. Multi-label classification tasks, such as assigning medical codes to clinical notes or determining news topics, are usually dominated by a large number of negatives, i.e., labels that do not apply. We show that existing binning schemes to compute label-wise expected calibration error either underestimate the error, simply reflect label frequency, or suffer from many bins with very few instances. To achieve trustworthy label-wise calibration errors, we propose a new binning scheme that gives equal weight to positive and negative label assignments. Our empirical study demonstrates that in contrast to existing binning schemes, our new scheme results in meaningful estimates of calibration error in hierarchical and in extreme multi-label classification. We also show that calibrating confidence scores of large language models for multi-label predictions is an open challenge. Our detailed analysis lays the foundation for further research by providing a solid evaluation metric for measuring calibration in multi-label classification.

</details>


### [127] [Spoken Language Models that Think Aloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26488)
*Junyi Ao,Kainan Peng,Mingbo Ma,Shun Zhang,Zhenyu Tang,Xutai Ma,Xiang Li,Yinghao Li,Yuancheng Wang,Zhizheng Wu,Haizhou Li,Qing He,Xubo Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种异步自我表达框架，以实现在链式推理语言模型中的实时对话，该框架通过动态平衡两种流的协调运行，减少语音间隙，同时保持答案准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的链式思维推理方法可能在口语模型中引入不必要的沉默间隔，影响实时互动。因此提出了此框架以提高口语模型的交互响应能力。

Method: 该框架包含一个主要推理流和一个轻量级的自我表达流。前者用于逻辑推理，后者根据用户输入和逐步发展的推理状态生成简短的任务相关进度说明。通过动态平衡策略在运行时协调这两种流，确保尽量减少沉默间隔。

Result: 实验结果显示，该方法显著减少了用户能听到的推理过程中的沉默间隔，并且保持了与传统的"思考后再说话"基线相当的答案准确性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了异步自我表达框架在提高口语推理语言模型的实时互动响应上的潜力。

Abstract: While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has improved the capability of language models, directly applying it to Spoken Language Models (SLMs) may introduce long silent intervals under the serial "think-then-speak" paradigm, disrupting real-time spoken interaction. To address this issue, we propose an asynchronous think-aloud framework for reasoning-based SLMs within the Thinker-Talker architecture. The framework maintains a primary reasoning stream for logical deduction and a lightweight think-aloud stream that generates short, task-grounded progress utterances conditioned on the user input and the evolving reasoning state. A dynamic balance strategy coordinates the two streams at runtime, triggering additional think-aloud speech to avoid silent gaps and canceling pending utterances when the final response becomes ready. Experiments on spoken reasoning and question-answering benchmarks show that our approach substantially reduces user-audible silence during reasoning while maintaining answer accuracy comparable to that of a serial "think-then-speak" baseline, demonstrating the potential of asynchronous think-aloud for responsive interaction in SLMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Do Synthetic Personas Predict Real Audience Response? A Sim-to-Real Study Where a No-Persona Baseline Beats Persona-Based Copy Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25010)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过使用Upworthy Research Archive的数据，发现使用合成人物进行预测的有效性低于直接询问模型，合成人物引入了偏差和噪声，降低了预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机是评估使用大型语言模型（LLMs）作为“合成人物”来预测目标受众对文案的反应是否有效。

Method: 研究采用了来自Upworthy Research Archive的实际A/B测试数据作为外部验证集，这些测试直接在实际用户流量上进行了比较。

Result: 结果发现，基于真实观众人口统计特征的合成人物未能提高预测准确性，实际上降低了预测效果。无合成人物的零样本模型表现更好。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论，直接使用LLM预测群体参与度的效果优于使用合成人物模型，合成人物不仅不是一个有效的预测工具，反而可能比不使用它们还要差。

Abstract: Marketers increasingly use large language models (LLMs) as "synthetic personas" to predict how an audience will react to a piece of copy before it ships, encouraged by evidence that profile-conditioned LLMs mimic human samples. But is that prediction actually valid against real behaviour - and does the persona machinery help? We present a sim-to-real validity study using the Upworthy Research Archive - thousands of headline A/B tests on shared real traffic, with measured click-through - as held-out ground truth. We compare a ten-persona panel, grounded in the real audience's demographics, against a no-persona zero-shot baseline that simply asks the model how likely a typical reader is to click. Two findings stand out. First, ground-truth reliability is the binding constraint: most A/B tests have no statistically distinguishable winner, so validity can only be measured on the reliable subset (n = 399). Second, and counter to the persona-simulation premise, persona conditioning degrades predictive validity: the no-persona baseline ranks variants markedly better (Kendall τ = 0.361, a medium effect; top-1 accuracy 49.2%) than the persona panel (τ = 0.084; top-1 34.6%), with non-overlapping confidence intervals. Asking the model directly taps an accurate population-level prior; forcing it to role-play specific personas injects bias and noise. The result replicates across three independent Upworthy splits, holds in direction on a different-domain news dataset, and is robust to seed, prompt phrasing, and model choice - across three Gemini tiers and a different model family (OpenAI gpt-4.1, significant paired gap). The takeaway: for predicting aggregate engagement, a plain LLM ranker beats persona simulation - synthetic personas are not merely a weak predictor, they are worse than not using them. All numbers regenerate from a public, artifact-first replication package.

</details>


### [129] [Do Existing Preconditioners Improve Biomedical Tabular Foundation Learning? An Empirical Study on TabPFN Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25013)
*M. Sajid,Pinki Khatun,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究对 TabPFN v2.5 进行了五种基于 AdamW 的预处理策略的全面实验，评估了其在多种生物医学数据集上的性能、计算效率和统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索优化和预处理策略对生物医学细调节 TabPFN v2.5 的影响，因为它在低数据量的生物医学表型分类任务中表现出强大的潜力。

Method: 通过使用五种 AdamW 基础的预处理策略对 TabPFN v2.5 进行生物医学数据集的细调节实验，涵盖了阿尔茨海默病、乳腺癌、精神分裂症、显著记忆力忧心 (SMC)、KEEL 生物医学数据集和 UCI 生物医学基准。

Result: 实验表明，原版 AdamW 优化器在所有评估指标上都表现出最佳的整体性能和统计排名，而现有的曲率感知预处理策略在不同的生物医学学习场景中未能提供可靠改进。

Conclusion: 研究发现，通用预处理方法可能无法充分捕捉生物医学表型学习中的优化特性，建议开发针对医疗导向的表型基础模型特定定制化预处理方法。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models have recently shown strong potential for structured biomedical data analysis. Among them, TabPFN has emerged as an effective approach for low-data tabular classification tasks. However, the impact of optimization and preconditioning strategies on biomedical fine-tuning remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present a comprehensive empirical investigation of five AdamW-based preconditioning strategies for fine-tuning TabPFN v2.5 on 59 biomedical datasets spanning Alzheimer's disease, breast cancer, schizophrenia, significant memory concern (SMC), KEEL biomedical datasets, and UCI biomedical benchmarks. The evaluation considers predictive performance, computational efficiency, and statistical significance analysis. Experimental results demonstrate that the original AdamW optimizer consistently achieves the best overall performance and statistical ranking, while existing curvature-aware preconditioners fail to provide reliable improvements across diverse biomedical learning scenarios. The findings suggest that generic preconditioning approaches may not adequately capture the optimization characteristics of biomedical tabular learning, motivating the development of biomedical-aware preconditioners specifically tailored for healthcare-oriented tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [130] [4DGS-JEPA: Temporally Compositional Joint-Embedding Prediction for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25036)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 4DGS-JEPA提出了一种用于动态高斯场景因果多时态预测的高斯中心联合嵌入预测架构，通过引入时空组成机制，改进预测状态兼容性、几何组成一致性和混合对应机制，以增强预测准确性和几何运动的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要集中在重建、后续状态生成或渲染，而对学习可重用的预测动力学优化不足。4DGS-JEPA旨在通过联合嵌入预测和时空组成机制，为动态高斯场景提供因果多时态预测的能力。

Method: 4DGS-JEPA利用分层场景、运动组和高斯级表示，结合时态条件下移位算子，支持直接预测和递归模拟。它通过锚定端点和多时态路径监督以及选择性几何解码器和几何级组成来确保预测的准确性与几何运动的一致性。

Result: 理论证明了零损失路径一致性并在实验中展示了改进的预测准确性和几何运动一致性，混合对应机制在增强身份性和鲁棒性方面表现良好。

Conclusion: 4DGS-JEPA为动态高斯世界提供了预测性、时空组成性的预测框架。

Abstract: Dynamic Gaussian Splatting provides an explicit representation of evolving 3D scenes, but existing approaches are primarily optimized for reconstruction, future-state generation, or rendering rather than for learning reusable predictive dynamics. We propose 4DGS-JEPA, a Gaussian-native joint-embedding predictive architecture for causal multi-horizon prediction over dynamic Gaussian scenes. The model uses a hierarchical scene-, motion-group-, and Gaussian-level representation together with a horizon-conditioned transition operator that supports both direct prediction and recursive rollout. Its central principle is temporal composition: different chronological transition paths reaching the same future endpoint should produce compatible predictive states. Endpoint and multi-horizon path supervision anchor these predictions to future target embeddings, while a selective geometry decoder and geometry-level composition ground the learned dynamics in consistent group motion and Gaussian geometry without requiring complete future appearance reconstruction. We further introduce a hybrid correspondence mechanism that combines persistent canonical identity with residual optimal-transport matching under reordering and topology change. We characterize zero-loss path agreement and finite-error rollout accumulation theoretically. Three controlled experiments provide mechanism-level evidence that temporal composition reduces latent path dependence while retaining predictive accuracy, geometry-level composition improves consistency of decoded motion, and hybrid correspondence preserves reliable identity while remaining robust when correspondence becomes ambiguous. Together, 4DGS-JEPA provides a predictive, temporally compositional formulation of dynamic Gaussian worlds.

</details>


### [131] [An Accurate and Interpretable Hyper Graph Neural Network for GBM Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25088)
*Mushahid Intesum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种多模态框架，利用鞘超图神经网络、概念瓶颈层和扩展充分性测试正则化器，从脑MRI中实现可解释的生存预测，该框架在UPenn-GBM数据集上达到了0.643的一致性指数，展示了最低的方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生存预测模型通常牺牲透明度以获得高性能，或者在提高解释性方面效果不佳。本文旨在通过一种多模态框架结合鞘超图神经网络、概念瓶颈层和扩展充分性测试正则化器来同时解决这两个目标。

Method: 该方法包括三个部分：鞘超图神经网络（用于捕捉组织块之间的高阶关系）、概念瓶颈层（压缩成临床可解释的概念）和扩展充分性测试正则化器（防止不忠实的解释）。临床和基因组特征通过门控融合方式集成，保持分子标记的主要预后信号，同时保持概念级别的可追溯性。

Result: 在593名UPenn-GBM数据集患者的数据上进行了5折交叉验证，该框架实现了0.643的一致性指数，展示了所有对比模型中最低的方差（标准差=0.015）。这是首次将鞘超图卷积、概念瓶颈监督和扩展充分性测试正则化统一应用于脑MRI中的可解释生存预测。

Conclusion: 该多模态框架能够同时提高生存预测的准确性与可解释性，在多模态MRI数据中实现了优异的表现，展示了对高级关系、概念压缩和解释性正则化的有效方法。

Abstract: Survival prediction for glioblastoma multiforme (GBM) demands models that are both accurate and interpretable, yet existing approaches treat these objectives as com- peting, where performant models sacrifice transparency, while interpretable models accept degraded predictive power. We argue that this trade-off is not inherent. Graph neural net- works offer a structural foundation for extracting interpretable, explainable representations without compromising discriminative ability. Furthermore, current methods typically rely on a single imaging modality, underutilizing the complementary information available across multi-modal MRI and clinical metadata. We propose a multi-modal framework that inte- grates three components to address both objectives simultaneously: (1) a sheaf hypergraph neural network that captures higher-order relationships among tissue patches through direc- tional, asymmetric message passing; (2) a concept bottleneck layer that compresses learned representations into clinically grounded concepts, enforcing ante-hoc interpretability; and (3) an extension sufficiency test (EST) regularizer that penalizes unfaithful explanations during training, ensuring that model explanations genuinely reflect the internal decision process. Clinical and genomic features are incorporated through gated fusion, preserving the dominant prognostic signal of molecular markers while retaining concept-level traceabil- ity. Evaluated on 593 patients from the UPenn-GBM dataset under 5-fold cross-validation, our framework achieves a concordance index of 0.643 with the lowest fold-level variance among all compared models (std = 0.015). To our knowledge, this is the first work to unify sheaf hypergraph convolution, concept bottleneck supervision, and EST regularization for interpretable survival prediction from brain MRI

</details>


### [132] [Ovis-Embedding: Pushing the Frontiers of Universal Omni-Modal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25165)
*Ovis-Embedding Team*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Ovis-Embedding 是一款高度集成的多模态嵌入模型，通过单一底层架构同时处理文本、图像、视频和音频信息。它通过预训练的 Qwen-omni 模型和对比学习，以及高效的数据采集和优化策略，实现了在多个任务上的顶尖性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在解决多模态数据处理中的模态碎片化问题，通过构建一个统一的嵌入空间来实现跨模态的高效检索。

Method: Ovis-Embedding 采用了预训练的 Qwen-omni 模型，并通过对比学习和低秩初始化进行适应性优化。数据采集方面，采用了均质源采样策略以提高数据利用效率。模型训练和推断过程中，使用了焦点损失和相似性嵌入蒸馏来增强模型的性能。在推断阶段，通过低秩特征分解实现了高效且灵活的嵌入。

Result: Ovis-Embedding 在 MMEB-v3、MMEB-v2、MVEB、MAEB 和 RTEB 等多个任务上取得了优于现有模型的性能，证明了多模态统一嵌入模型的有效性。

Conclusion: Ovis-Embedding 的成功展示了统一多模态培训方法的潜力，为开发适用于任何到任何检索任务的嵌入模型铺平了道路。

Abstract: In this report, we introduce \textbf{Ovis-Embedding}, a state-of-the-art omni-modal embedding family built on native integration of text, image, video, and audio. Instead of assembling separate modality towers, Ovis-Embedding uses a shared multimodal backbone to encode different modalities in a common representation space. Specifically, we make \textbf{three key advances}: (1) \textbf{native omni-modal initialization}: we adopt a pretrained Qwen-omni model as the embedding backbone and adapt it through contrastive training with low-rank initialization; (2) \textbf{data-centric omni-modal training}: we construct a broad, high-quality corpus spanning text, images, video, audio, and interleaved multimodal data. To improve data efficiency, we introduce homogeneous-source sampling to form task-consistent batches with informative in-batch negatives; and (3) \textbf{embedding-specific training and inference optimization}: we use focal loss to emphasize hard examples and similarity-based Embedding Distillation to transfer fine-grained similarity structure from complementary experts. At inference time, low-rank feature decomposition enables compact embeddings with flexible dimensionality and minimal performance loss. Empirical evaluations show that the \textbf{Ovis-Embedding} family achieves state-of-the-art performance on \textbf{MMEB-v3}, \textbf{MMEB-v2}, \textbf{MVEB}, \textbf{MAEB}, and \textbf{RTEB}, demonstrating its effectiveness across text, image, video, and audio modalities. These results highlight the potential of unified omni-modal training to overcome modality fragmentation and advance universal embedding models for any-to-any retrieval.

</details>


### [133] [AgenticSizing: A Large Language Model-based Multi-Agent Framework for Analog Circuit Sizing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25873)
*Yijia Hao,Pratibha Verma,Dongxu Guo,Cristian Sestito,Michael O'Boyle,Christos-Savvas Bouganis,Themis Prodromakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体的大规模语言模型框架，用于复杂模拟电路的尺寸优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决模拟电路设计中由于设计空间庞大、性能权衡紧密以及电路复杂度增加所带来的挑战，以及现有基于大规模语言模型的方法在强电路拓扑理解能力上的不足。

Method: 该框架首先分析电路拓扑并分解为功能模块和子结构，提取轻量级设计知识供重复使用。基于此拓扑和知识，调度器协调多个角色专业化的尺寸代理，更新设计变量，以实现全局性能规格。此外，还通过消融研究证明了拓扑理解、设计知识注入和智能体专业化提供的补充益处。

Result: 该方法在八个电路中进行了验证，最大的设计包括多达55个晶体管和60个尺寸变量。在LDO基准测试中，该方法的平均迭代数为83次，成功率达到了60%。同时，经典优化器未能找到可行的解决方案。

Conclusion: 提出的框架提供了一种结构化、可解释的、基于仿真的优化过程，能够有效地进行复杂模拟电路尺寸优化。

Abstract: Analog circuit sizing remains a challenging and time-consuming task due to the large design space, strong performance trade-offs, and increasing circuit complexity in scaled technologies. Although recent large language model (LLM)-based methods show promise in improving sample efficiency and interpretability, existing approaches often lack explicit circuit-topology understanding and are mainly evaluated on relatively simple analog building blocks. This paper presents a multi-agent LLM-based framework for complex analog circuit sizing. The proposed framework first analyzes the circuit topology and decomposes the netlist into functional blocks and substructures. It also extracts lightweight design knowledge for reuse. Based on the extracted topology and knowledge, a planner coordinates multiple role-specialized sizing agents to update design variables and achieve global performance specifications. This workflow mimics the collaborative process of an expert analog design team and provides a structured, interpretable, and simulation-driven optimization procedure. The framework was validated on eight circuits, with the largest design containing up to 55 transistors and 60 sizing variables. Notably, for the LDO benchmark, the proposed method achieved a 60\% success rate with an average of 83 iterations, where classical optimizers failed to find feasible solutions. Further, ablation studies demonstrate that topology understanding, design-knowledge infusion, and agent specialization provide complementary benefits. The source code is available to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [134] [X-Planner: Event-Structured Task Planning for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25187)
*Howard Lu,Shalfun Li,Porter Pan,Cris,Lumen,Cyril,Eric Hu,Lily Li,Maeve Zhang,Robert Wang,KZ Zheng,Viggo Chen,Tim Ding,Regsis Cheng,YJ Xiao,Kian,Hai Lin,Alan Song,Elise Ma,Gody Li,Victor Yao,Yohann Tang,Ingrid Yu,Jason He,James Wang,Ryan Yu,Ping Yang,Chris Pan,Vincent Chen,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X-Planner通过引入层次化的监督和嵌入式推理表示，解决了现代VLA系统的透明规划问题，实现了高质量的计划文本和下游执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLA系统在长期操作中的任务规划往往缺乏明确的中间结构，而现有的CoT规划方法则依赖粗粒度的任务注释或逐字的长推理记录。

Method: X-Planner采用一种层次化的监督方法，并通过Ego、UMI和遥控操作的混合数据源进行注释，同时使用自动化失败和时间接管标注来监督错误识别。

Result: X-Planner在离线评估中得分高，特别是在BERTScore-F1和基于评审员的整体评分中排名第二，并且在真实机器人实验中优于基线模型，展示了高质量的计划文本和下游执行。

Conclusion: X-Planner通过引入复杂的规划前端，为VLA系统提供了一个有效的解决方案，改善了任务规划和实际执行的性能。

Abstract: Task planning bridges high-level instructions and executable behavior in long-horizon manipulation, yet modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems often leave this intermediate structure implicit. Existing chain-of-thought (CoT) planners also tend to rely on coarse task-level annotations or serialize long reasoning traces token by token. We present X-Planner, a planning front-end that addresses both the supervision and representation of embodied reasoning. Our planning data combine Ego, UMI, and teleoperation under a hierarchy granularity with source-dependent annotation depth. Takeover-time annotations and human-designed failures supervise ongoing error recognition. On the model side, a shared VLM backbone exposes two event-structured plan forms: a discrete interface that emits interpretable event states and a latent interface that relays continuous CoT states across staggered Transformer depths through Staircase Decoding. A frozen latent-to-text reconstruction objective provides a semantic anchor for the latent representation. Offline two-step planning evaluation places X-Planner second among four evaluated models on both BERTScore-F1 and a judge-based Overall score. In real-robot experiments, respectively, outperforming the evaluated baselines. These results characterize planning-text quality and downstream execution.

</details>


### [135] [Lean Pool: An AI-Maintained Archive of Formalized Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25199)
*Vasily Ilin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lean Pool是一种形式化的数学存储库，由AI代理培育、维护和优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未在摘要中明确提及动机，但可以推测其目的是利用AI技术提高数学形式化的效率和准确性。

Method: 未在摘要中详细说明具体方法，推测是通过AI代理自动化地贡献、验证和优化形式化的数学内容。

Result: 未提及具体结果。

Conclusion: 未提及结论。

Abstract: Lean Pool is a repository of formalized mathematics. It is grown, maintained and optimized by AI agents.

</details>


### [136] [The AI Neuroscientist: An Interactive Agentic Interface for Neuroimaging Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25254)
*Aakash Patel,Panos Ketonis,Shreya Saxena,Smita Krishnaswamy,David van Dijk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为AI神经科学家的语言代理，该代理结合了大规模语言模型和神经成像工具集，用于质量控制、建模和可视化，以实现神经成像数据的交互式探索，提升了非计算机背景的研究者的数据处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前分析神经影像学数据需要专门的编程和统计知识，这限制了缺乏计算机背景的科研工作者的可访问性。AI神经科学家的目的是通过语言交互的方式，让研究者能够直接用自然语言查询数据质量和指定分析参数，提供一个透明且交互式的管线解决方案。

Method: AI神经科学家采用了大规模语言模型与神经影像学工具集的集成方式，支持数据质量控制、建模和可视化功能，实现了自然语言输入与结果输出的直接对接。

Result: 该系统通过fNIRS数据演示了其功能，并与通用语言模型代理进行了对比，结果显示AI神经科学家在小型数据探索中提供了更透明和交互的体验。

Conclusion: 未来，该架构计划扩展到其他模态的数据处理，如fMRI数据，并将基准测试套件扩展到更多的fNIRS任务。

Abstract: Analyzing neuroimaging data requires specialized coding and statistical expertise, which limits accessibility for researchers without computational backgrounds. We present the AI Neuroscientist, a language agent for interactive data exploration. The system integrates a large language model (LLM) with a neuroimaging toolset to perform quality control, modeling, and visualization. This allows researchers to query data quality and specify analysis parameters directly in natural language, providing a transparent and interactive alternative to conventional scripted pipelines for small-scale data exploration. We demonstrate these capabilities using functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) data, and evaluate the agent on a custom fNIRS benchmarking suite against general-purpose LLM agents with code sandboxes. Future extensions will generalize the architecture to additional modalities, including functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data, and expand the benchmarking suite to additional fNIRS tasks.

</details>


### [137] [MedGate-Fusion: Integrating First-Encounter Semantic Narratives and Physiological Biomarkers for Prospective Stroke Risk Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25272)
*Hemn Khdr,Mohammad Noaeen,Karim Keshavjee,Aziz Guergachi,Zahra Shakeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedGate-Fusion 是一种多模态门控架构，结合了基于变换器的首次就诊叙述嵌入和十个常规记录的风险指标，用于初级保健环境中的中风风险分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在初级保健中，中风风险信号分散在常规生物标志物和非结构化临床叙述中，使得风险分层具有挑战性。MedGate-Fusion 的提出旨在克服这一困难。

Method: 该方法使用电子医疗记录数据，首先从808,921个首次就诊观察中构建了一个首次就诊队列，然后通过字典方法删除了与中风相关的术语，最后结合首次就诊叙述的变换器嵌入和十个常规风险指标。

Result: 成功构建了首次就诊队列，并保留了102,736个具有非空叙述和足够数据以评估五年中风结果的唯一患者记录。

Conclusion: 通过这种方法，可以从未经过滤的叙述文本和常规风险指标中更准确地识别中风风险，这表明该模型在实际临床中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Prospective stroke risk stratification in primary care is challenging because early risk signals are distributed across routine biomarkers and unstructured clinical narratives. We propose MedGate-Fusion, a multi-modal gated architecture that integrates transformer-based embeddings of first-encounter narratives with ten routinely recorded risk markers. We used electronic medical record data from the Canadian Primary Care Sentinel Surveillance Network (CPCSSN). Starting from 808,921 encounter-level observations, we constructed a first-encounter cohort and retained 102,736 unique patient records with non-empty narratives and sufficient data to evaluate a five-year stroke outcome. To reduce explicit target leakage from diagnostic mentions in notes, we applied dictionary-based redaction of stroke-related terms prior to semantic encoding.

</details>


### [138] [When LLM Agents Fail to Read the Room: ReAdapt for Relational Social Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25284)
*Jianzhe Lin,Xiaolin Li,Yunda Liu,Fei Wang,Jubin Chheda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究指出传统LLM代理模型在处理社交决策时，未能有效利用关系信息进行更新，从而导致了表面选择倾向。为此，提出了一种名为ReAdapt的模型，通过引入结构化社会状态来增强代理的决策过程，从而显著提高了任务完成的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM代理在处理社交情境下的决策时，过分依赖表面信息，而忽视了重要的关系证据。作者认为，有效地利用关系线索对于做出更合理的社交决策至关重要。

Method: 为了验证这一假设，作者构建了关系推理基准测试，包含500个合成的社交世界，并在此基础上提出了ReAdapt模型来提高代理的社会交互能力。

Result: 实验结果显示，ReAdapt在社交引荐任务中的准确率从37%提升至51%，在帖子反应选择任务中的准确率从69%提升至77%；此外，代理对错误选择所表现出的遗憾程度分别减少了33.08%和43.90%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，通过引入结构化社会状态并利用关系信息进行推理，可以显著改善LLM代理在社交决策中的表现。

Abstract: A social agent's most basic decisions (should I react to this post? who should I reach out to?) are not purely content problems. The right action often hinges on the latent relationship between people -- tie strength, reciprocity, mutual connections -- rather than on which content is most salient. Standard LLM agent loops do not explicitly represent how new relational evidence should revise the agent's current social hypothesis, leaving them prone to surface-obvious choices when relational and content cues diverge. We formalize this failure mode with a relationship-reasoning benchmark: 500 synthetic social worlds with friendships, follows, reaction histories, and feeds, yielding 1,000 queries over two tasks, reaction selection and warm introduction (finding the best bridge to a target person). By construction, the surface-obvious candidate differs from the relationship-grounded oracle in about 53% of queries, forming an overturn subset where the agent must use relational evidence to revise an initially plausible choice. We propose ReAdapt (Relationship-Adaptive Agent with Policy-driven sTate), which augments the ReAct loop with an explicit structured social state z = (G, B, R, N, D) capturing goal, belief, relationship, norm, and disclosure. After each tool observation, ReAdapt runs a typed Adapt step that updates this state and emits a policy operation (continue, switch, abandon, or clarify) before choosing the next action. With Gemini-3-Flash on a stratified subset of n = 150 queries per task, ReAdapt improves warm-introduction accuracy from 37% to 51% (+14 points) and reaction-selection accuracy from 69% to 77% (+8 points). Oracle regret drops from 0.260 to 0.152 and from 0.095 to 0.053, respectively. Holding the model, tools, and environments fixed, these results suggest that explicit relational-state adaptation helps LLM agents turn retrieved social evidence into revised decisions.

</details>


### [139] [Attention as a Routing Graph: Live Circuit Extraction from a Single Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25285)
*Ash Manvi,Samreena Tajreen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种简化方法来检测语言模型中的路径，通过保留指向答案的一小部分路径，发现这些路径通常对模型表现有显著影响，特别是在已知正确路径的任务上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的寻找语言模型中的路径方法大多是通过多次精确干预，而该研究尝试了一种更简单的方法来检测这些路径，以降低成本并验证路径的有效性。

Method: 研究团队将注意力视为从一个前向传递到另一端节点的路由图，保留一些指向正确答案的路径，并通过对比这些路径和随机路径的影响来检测路径的因果信号。

Result: 实验结果表明，与随机路径相比，从模型中提取的路径对模型性能的负面影响更大。该方法在多种基准测试上进行了评估，包括GPT-2 Small、GPT-2 Medium和Pythia-410M，并且结果显示，经过选择的路径确实携带实际的因果信号。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，尽管该方法可能不提供完整的电路图谱，但可以生成一种成本较低的路径草图，并在已知任务上有效验证因果信号，具有明确的失败模式指示器。

Abstract: Finding circuits in language models usually means running many careful interventions. We try something simpler: treat attention as a routing map from one forward pass, keep a small set of routes that point toward the answer, and ask whether those routes actually matter.
  They often do. On induction and IOI (tasks where the "right" circuit is already known), ablating our extracted edges hurts the model much more than ablating a random set of the same size. We evaluate n=100 prompts per cell on GPT-2 Small, GPT-2 Medium, and Pythia-410M, with paired gap tests and bootstrap confidence intervals. The extract step costs one forward; a head-by-head patch sweep costs about two orders of magnitude more.
  We are not claiming a complete circuit atlas. We are claiming a cheap sketch that carries real causal signal on known tasks, with clear failure modes when it does not. Code and evaluation artifacts are at https://github.com/Aquinf03/live-circuit-routing.

</details>


### [140] [Learned Enterprise Data Comprehension: Compression and Routing for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25286)
*Ethan Torres,Eric Mills*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了潜在等价学习框架，将持久的任务相关身份与其数据集相对实现分离，支持证据和反对证据共同作用以学习身份在特定数据环境中的表达方式，同时软成员身份保留了硬分配中丢失的差异。通过学习查询原型系统和兼容函数，该框架能够根据不同查询条件的数据库特定证据进行推理，从而允许代理基于已组织的证据状态操作，而不是在每次查询时重建跨模式结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的智能代理系统在处理分布于多个模式、关系、策略和业务角色中的复杂数据环境时负担沉重，而该研究旨在弥合理性推理与预测关系组织之间的差距，以提高复杂数据环境下的代理性能。

Method: 研究提出了一个潜在等价学习框架，该框架将持久的任务相关身份与其数据集相对实现分离。通过融合支持证据和反对证据，学习在特定数据环境中的表达方式，并通过一个学习查询原型系统及其关联的兼容函数，将查询条件与持久身份结构进行匹配，从而实现基于已组织的证据状态的推理。

Result: 在跨54个查询和12个异构数据集的Data Agent Benchmark上，该框架的全实现达到了94.67%的数据宏分层通过率（Pass@1，五个完整试次的总体性能），并成功处理了258/270个原始查询请求，远超基准中的Claude Opus 4.6参考代理的55.51%通过率，展现了在复杂数据环境下的优越性能。

Conclusion: 这一研究展示了如何通过潜在等价学习框架消除代理在复杂数据环境中重建结构的负担，同时保持对特定任务相关身份的识别与组织，为提高代理在企业级数据环境中的表现提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Structured-data agents in enterprise settings must reason over complex data environments whose relevant evidence is distributed across schemas, relationships, policies, and recurring business roles. Modern agentic systems often address this burden through reusable markdown-style memory or skill files that preserve previously discovered information for later queries, reducing the need to rediscover the same structure repeatedly. This is useful, but it obscures a natural division of labor: agents are well suited to semantic reasoning, while learned systems are well suited to predicting and organizing recurring structure. We introduce latent equivalence learning to bridge this gap. The framework separates persistent task-relevant identities from their dataset-relative realizations. In our realization, supporting and opposing evidence shape support-realized Gaussian prototypes that learn how those identities are expressed in a particular data environment, while soft-membership profiles retain distinctions lost under a hard assignment. A separate learned query-prototype system represents recurring evidential requirements and maps them through a learned compatibility function into the same persistent identity structure. This identity-factorized, query-conditioned routing materializes the relevant dataset-specific evidence for downstream reasoning, allowing the agent to operate over an already organized evidential state rather than reconstructing cross-schema structure at every query. On the Data Agent Benchmark, spanning 54 queries across 12 heterogeneous datasets, our full implementation achieves 94.67% dataset-macro stratified Pass@1 over five complete trials and 258/270 successful raw query attempts, compared with 55.51% for the benchmark's Claude Opus 4.6 reference agent, ranking first among 40 leaderboard entries at submission.

</details>


### [141] [Making Agents More Consistent: Skills Should Form Habits for Repeat Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25299)
*Travis Weber,Rohit Taneja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在重复任务中，代理并不一致，并且存在浪费现象。本文提出了一种技能习惯形成的方法，通过利用代理自身的执行历史来识别候选技能，这些技能竞争而非替代现状。这在多次重复的文本到SQL任务上提高了系统的一致性，并减少了计算资源的使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高机器代理的一致性和效率，特别是在关键任务场景下如买家、审计员或监管者的使用需求。

Method: 作者通过让代理从自身执行历史中挖掘候选技能，并在一个包含多个分级阈值的系统中进行竞争，从而形成习惯。这些候选技能在常见情况下会自动执行，而在特殊场景下则进行推理。

Result: 在文本到SQL的转化任务上，四种推理路径中有三种在重复的42个问题中重用了11到13次，第四种重用了26次，而习得的习惯性版本在重复的456次任务中无一失败，并且在大多数情况下比被替换的模型使用更少的计算资源。

Conclusion: 此研究结论认为，通过形成具有自我检查和竞争机制的习惯性技能，可以提高机器代理的一致性和效率，并在此过程中提高了整体系统准确度。

Abstract: On repeated work, agents are inconsistent. We ran 42 tasks three times each and found that, depending on the model, 38% to 74% returned answers that did not agree. Consistency is what a buyer, an auditor, or a regulator requires, and agents do not have it. They are wasteful too: 95.3% to 97.2% of what an agent generates goes to re-deriving a plan the system already knows.
  We propose skill habit formation. An agent mines its own execution history for candidate skills, deterministic variants that compete against the incumbent rather than replacing it. A candidate declares the region of input space it claims, so the common case runs as a script and the rest falls through to reasoning. Four gates of ascending cost admit candidates; the central one tests a candidate's execution trace against a retained reference, within a tolerance measured from that reference's own run-to-run variability.
  On text-to-SQL, three of four reasoning arms reproduced their own output on 11 to 13 of 42 repeated questions and the fourth on 26 of 42, while a habit-formed variant reproduced on all 456 dispatches we repeated and was non-inferior to every arm it replaced (p<0.0001). It also used 14% to 56% fewer tokens, turning net positive after 7 to 53 reuses.
  We measured what this costs in accuracy. The guard admitted work it should have deferred on 2.6% of natural paraphrases and 26% of inputs near its boundary, and 11 of 13 such failures were invisible to the trace-conformance gate at any threshold. Deterministic errors repeat exactly: a bad habit is as reliable as a good one, and that is the price of the property that makes the system auditable. Separating routing from parameter extraction raised end-to-end accuracy from 0.888 to 0.952 at 43% of the cost.

</details>


### [142] [Potential for Enhanced Learning in Machine Learning Classes by Using Wiki LLM Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25303)
*Brian Wright*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究表明，在同一课程语料库上使用两种不同的知识表示方法，发现基于维基的结构在跨单元概念链接方面表现更好，更准确且更加扎实地引用了原始材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为探讨课程特定的大型语言模型在使用经过验证的教学材料时，语料库结构比查询时的检索策略更为重要。

Method: 将一个课程语料库分成两种不同的知识表示方法进行对比：一种是向量化的RAG系统，另一种是以Karpathy框架为基础的维基，后者在语料库摄入时将其合成到链接概念页面，并明确引用回来源材料。

Result: 对于单个事实的回忆，两种方法表现相差不大；但在需要跨单元概念链接的问题上，维基的合成结构表现出色，并且准确且扎实地引用了原始材料。而基于检索的方法则表现较弱，且难以追溯到原始材料。

Conclusion: 研究建议，使用基于维基的结构可能有助于支持机器学习课程中最佳实践的基础元素。虽有待进一步测试，但使用维基结构的大型语言模型可以为学习者和教师提供对讲义中任何陈述的有效追溯，增强了动态检索的支持。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as course-specific tutors, but their usefulness depends on grounding in vetted instructional materials that are often revised mid-semester. Our prior work built a multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system over an authentic machine learning course corpus (Foundations of Machine Learning) and found that retrieval improved contextual grounding, but that fixed retrieval strategies were suboptimal. That motivates a different question: whether how a corpus is structured at ingest time matters more than how much is retrieved at query time. We present a controlled head-to-head comparison of two knowledge representations over an identical classroom corpus: (A) vector RAG, replicating the best-performing configuration from our prior study, and (B) an LLM-compiled wiki (Karpathy framework), in which the corpus is synthesized at ingest into linked concept pages with explicit cross-references and citations back to source materials. We evaluate 59 questions spanning single-fact recall, cross-unit concept linking, synthesis and explanation, and currency after a syllabus revision, scored by an LLM judge against a human-authored rubric. Both representations answered single-fact questions about equally well (9.33 vs. 9.96 of 10), but diverged sharply on questions requiring links across course units. The compiled wiki remained accurate and grounded (9.93; 100% grounded in cited sources), while retrieval scored lower and was markedly less grounded (8.14; 64%). The wiki's citations let students and instructors trace any claim back to the lecture that introduced it, adding a layer of dynamic retrieval that machine learning courses require. While further testing is needed, instructors using AI to support learning in ML courses should consider wiki-based structure for its potential to support foundational elements of best practice.

</details>


### [143] [Clarification Is Not Correction: LLMs Fail to Let Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25337)
*Jianzhe Lin,Xiaolin Li,Fei Wang,Robert Douglas,Rajeshkumar Golani,Jubin Chheda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出了语言模型在对话中常见的早期假定问题，即对话模型过早地对模糊的用户意图做出假设，即使后期有澄清，这种假设也难以改变。研究者使用Gemini-2.5-Pro和Gemini-2.5-Flash进行了多项实验，结果显示任务顺序的不同会对结果产生显著影响。论文提出了保持不确定性状态管理的重要性，并建议助手应保持模糊意图的假设，直到证据明确，从而提高交互式LLM的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在解决现有对话模型中对话过早收敛的问题，即模型在面对用户模糊意图时过早地做出假设，导致后续的澄清信息无法改变这种状态。

Method: 研究者设计了控制对话任务，包括写作、计划和编程任务，并使用Gemini-2.5-Pro和Gemini-2.5-Flash两个模型进行了大量实验。

Result: 实验结果显示，任务信息的不同顺序能够导致不同的结果，即便是最终的对话包含了等效的任务相关信息。这表明后续的澄清信息被视为额外的上下文信息而不是纠正信号：它仅仅细化了已过时的任务状态而不予以撤销。

Conclusion: 论文得出结论，标准提示和记忆策略不足以解决早期假设问题。研究者建议助手应保留模糊意图的假设，直到形成确切证据为止，并在高影响的模糊情况发生时请求确认，必要时重建新的状态。

Abstract: Dialogue failures in language models are usually framed as memory failures: context too long, summaries lossy, a constraint forgotten. We argue this misses a deeper problem: in many conversations the model does not forget, it commits too early. An ambiguous early turn collapses into a single hidden interpretation, and later clarification is filtered through that commitment. We call this early posterior collapse: unresolved user intent collapsing into a committed task state before ambiguity is resolved. We study it with controlled dialogue tasks in writing, planning, and coding using Gemini-2.5-Pro and Gemini-2.5-Flash. Across thousands of trials, the same information in different orders yields different outcomes, even when the final dialogue contains equivalent task-relevant information. This order effect suggests later clarification is treated as extra context rather than a corrective signal: it refines a stale task state without invalidating it. Coding tasks are especially vulnerable, suggesting early assumptions get embedded in structured artifacts such as interfaces and control flow. Standard prompting and memory strategies do not reliably help: summaries can collapse ambiguity, and chain-of-thought can reduce explicit wrong commitment in reasoning traces without improving final task success. These findings motivate uncertainty-preserving state management. If assistants cannot let go of early interpretations, robustness cannot rely on post hoc correction alone; it must keep ambiguous early turns from hardening into one task state. Assistants should hold tentative hypotheses while ambiguity remains, ask before executing when high-impact ambiguity persists, and rebuild from a revised state when later evidence invalidates an earlier reading. Rather than one prompting fix, we aim to redirect research for interactive LLMs from retaining more context toward preserving uncertainty.

</details>


### [144] [From Decorative to Load-Bearing: Task Difficulty Shapes the Causal Role of Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25366)
*Renee Jia,Di Mu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文引入了一种因果测试方法，研究链式推理负载性与模型任务难度的关系，发现任务难度显著影响推理错误的传递性，而因果测试对这种影响的敏感性较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的链式推理监测方法关注的是机械上的忠实性，而非行为上的因果关系。本文旨在找到一种能够评估推理负载性（即推理步骤对最终答案的重要性）的方法，该方法可以更加有效地区分不同难度的任务和它们对推理错误的敏感度。

Method: 论文提出了延续基于的因果测试方法，通过干预单个推理步骤并截短推理链，迫使模型从误导性前缀继续推理，从而评估推理步骤的重要性。

Result: 实验结果发现，任务难度在很大程度上决定了推理错误是否会传递；具体而言，从GSM8K到BBH多步骤算术任务，推理错误传递性提升了16倍；并且98.8%的变异差异可归因于任务难度，而非测试干扰类型。

Conclusion: 研究指出，虽然因果测试可以识别推理负载性，但这并没有解决AI安全监测中遇到的结构问题，即当推理容易被理解时，它可能不携带重要信号；而在需要干预时，已发生的推理错误却已经传递给下游，使得监督变得困难。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is only meaningful if written reasoning causally constrains the answer. We introduce continuation-based causal testing, an ablation-patch intervention that perturbs one reasoning step, truncates the chain, and forces the model to continue from the corrupted prefix. It measures how load-bearing a CoT is for the final answer, a behavioral notion distinct from mechanistic faithfulness. Across Gemma-2-9B-IT, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B on GSM8K, MMLU, and BIG-Bench Hard, CoT load-bearingness tracks model-relative task difficulty: on easy tasks models silently bypass their own reasoning; on hard tasks they follow corrupted steps and propagate errors. A matched 2x2 analysis shows task difficulty dominates perturbation type: error propagation rises 16x from GSM8K to BBH multistep arithmetic, and a variance partition over 28,584 continuations attributes 98.8% of explained deviance to task difficulty versus 0.8% to perturbation type. Reasoning-specific RL suppresses error propagation and compresses the gradient. A four-variant judge-sensitivity analysis and blind two-annotator study (n=500) show the error-propagation vs. non-propagation label is invariant to judge prompt, with perfect inter-annotator agreement (Cohen's kappa = 1.00). This gradient creates a structural problem for CoT-based oversight and AI safety monitoring: where the trace is easy to read it carries little signal, and where it matters errors propagate before a monitor can intervene. Linear probes on hidden states separate silent bypass, self-correction, and error propagation, but additive activation steering provides limited causal control, flipping only about 25% of error-propagation cases at best. Behavioral mode is readable but not reliably controllable.

</details>


### [145] [Robust Failure, Conservative Repair: Textual Knowledge Distillation from Cross-Model Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25400)
*Andrew Ren,Haokun Liu,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过定义规则原子来改善失败引导的知识蒸馏方法，提出了一种鲁棒失败保守修复(RFCR)的文本蒸馏过程，该过程从多个模型的共同错误中提取规则，并通过边界案例精确规则的应用范围，使基准提示表在BIG-Bench Hard任务集上的正确率提高了2.75个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于失败的知识蒸馏方法在不同类型模型之间的知识转移不够稳定，因此研究提出了鲁棒失败保守修复(RFCR)方法，以改善知识蒸馏效果。

Method: 研究首先定义了规则原子的概念，通过将规则原子注入模型的推理过程中，从多个模型共享的错误中提取规则，并且使用边界案例确定规则的应用范围。

Result: 在BIG-Bench Hard任务集上，RFCR方法将基准提示表的正确率从68.50%提高到了71.25%，提高了2.75个百分点；同时在已正确预测的案例上不会降低性能。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地通过鲁棒失败保守修复方法提高了知识蒸馏的效果，并且表明这种方法的有效性主要来源于新知识的注入和规则应用控制的严格性。

Abstract: Failure-based textual knowledge distillation aims to discover gaps in a model's knowledge by examining its task errors. The distilled knowledge can be useful for the reasoning of both this model ("source model") and other models. However, this transfer of knowledge may not be stable. We define a rule atom to be a standalone rule injected into a model's textual input at inference time. A rule atom can encode transferable task knowledge or model-specific reasoning patches that can confuse other models. Also, the injected rule atoms can be misapplied to unrelated cases, causing the model to incorrectly flip its answer based on irrelevant information. Building on a pipeline that distills training examples into task-specific cheat sheets that aid model reasoning, we examine when failure-derived rules can improve these cheat sheets.
